---
title: 期权OI墙
date: 2026-08-13
subtitle: 科技股周度期权持仓量(OI)分布，按行权价拉ladder
summary: 选一个标的，看期权持仓量在各行权价的真实分布，CALL/PUT分开展示，TOP3持仓行权价高亮标出，默认只看现价±35%范围内的行权价。
---

<div class="summary-box">
科技股周度期权OI（未平仓合约量）分布图。选一个标的，切换到期日和CALL/PUT方向，就能看到持仓量在各行权价的分布ladder，TOP3持仓最集中的行权价会高亮标出（通常对应机构重仓的关键价位，可以作为支撑/压力参考）。默认只展示现价±35%范围内的行权价，可以展开看全部。
</div>

<style>
.oi-wall {
  --call: #C0392B; --call-l: #E8B8B0; --call-top: #7A1F14;
  --put: #2F7D5C; --put-l: #B8D8C8; --put-top: #154D34;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
}
.oi-wall *, .oi-wall *::before, .oi-wall *::after { box-sizing: border-box; }

.oi-wall .wrap { max-width: 100%; margin: 0; padding: 0; }

.oi-wall .hero {
  background: var(--bg);
  padding: 20px 18px 18px;
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: 10px;
  margin-bottom: 16px;
}
.oi-wall .hero-eyebrow {
  font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 11px; letter-spacing: 0.1em;
  color: var(--accent); text-transform: uppercase; margin-bottom: 8px;
}
.oi-wall .hero-title {
  font-family: var(--font-sans); font-weight: 700; font-size: 22px; color: var(--ink);
  line-height: 1.25; margin-bottom: 6px;
}
.oi-wall .hero-title em { font-style: italic; color: var(--accent); }
.oi-wall .hero-sub { font-size: 13px; color: var(--muted); }
.oi-wall .hero-meta {
  margin-top: 14px; display: flex; gap: 10px; flex-wrap: wrap;
  font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 11px; color: var(--muted);
}
.oi-wall .hero-meta span { background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--border) 30%, var(--bg)); padding: 3px 10px; border-radius: 20px; border: 1px solid var(--border); }

.oi-wall .picker-section { padding: 0 0 16px; }
.oi-wall .search-box {
  width: 100%; padding: 10px 14px; border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 8px;
  font-family: var(--font-sans); font-size: 14px; background: var(--bg); color: var(--ink);
  margin-bottom: 12px; outline: none;
}
.oi-wall .search-box:focus { border-color: var(--accent); }
.oi-wall .chip-row { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 6px; margin-bottom: 4px; }
.oi-wall .chip {
  font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 12.5px; padding: 6px 12px; border-radius: 8px;
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--border) 25%, var(--bg)); border: 1px solid var(--border); color: var(--ink);
  cursor: pointer; transition: all .12s ease; user-select: none;
}
.oi-wall .chip:hover { border-color: var(--accent); }
.oi-wall .chip.active { background: var(--accent); border-color: var(--accent); color: var(--bg); }
.oi-wall .chip.hidden { display: none; }

.oi-wall .panel { padding: 0; }
.oi-wall .panel-empty { padding: 50px 16px; text-align: center; color: var(--muted); font-size: 13px; }

.oi-wall .symbol-head { display: flex; align-items: baseline; gap: 10px; margin-bottom: 4px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.oi-wall .symbol-head h2 { font-family: var(--font-sans); font-weight: 700; font-size: 20px; color: var(--ink); }
.oi-wall .symbol-head .price { font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 13px; color: var(--muted); }

.oi-wall .expiry-tabs { display: flex; gap: 6px; margin: 12px 0 16px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.oi-wall .expiry-tab {
  font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 11.5px; padding: 6px 12px; border-radius: 8px;
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--border) 25%, var(--bg)); border: 1px solid var(--border); color: var(--muted); cursor: pointer;
}
.oi-wall .expiry-tab.active { background: var(--accent); border-color: var(--accent); color: var(--bg); }

.oi-wall .stat-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr); gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 20px; }
.oi-wall .stat-box { background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--border) 25%, var(--bg)); border-radius: 8px; padding: 10px 8px; text-align: center; }
.oi-wall .stat-label { font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 9px; color: var(--muted); text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.04em; margin-bottom: 3px; }
.oi-wall .stat-val { font-family: var(--font-sans); font-weight: 700; font-size: 15px; color: var(--ink); }
.oi-wall .stat-box.ratio .stat-val { color: var(--accent); }

.oi-wall .oi-tabs { display: flex; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 14px; }
.oi-wall .oi-tab {
  flex: 1; font-family: var(--font-sans); font-weight: 700; font-size: 13px; padding: 10px;
  border-radius: 8px; text-align: center; cursor: pointer; border: 2px solid transparent;
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--border) 25%, var(--bg)); color: var(--muted); transition: all .12s ease;
}
.oi-wall .oi-tab .n { display: block; font-family: var(--font-mono); font-weight: 400; font-size: 10px; margin-top: 2px; opacity: .8; }
.oi-wall .oi-tab.call.active { background: var(--call); color: #fff; border-color: var(--call-top); }
.oi-wall .oi-tab.put.active { background: var(--put); color: #fff; border-color: var(--put-top); }

.oi-wall .legend { display: flex; gap: 16px; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 10px; font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 10.5px; color: var(--muted); flex-wrap: wrap; }
.oi-wall .legend-item { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 5px; }
.oi-wall .legend-dot { width: 10px; height: 10px; border-radius: 3px; }
.oi-wall .legend-dot.regular { opacity: .55; }
.oi-wall .legend-dot.top { border: 2px solid #E8B84A; }

.oi-wall .ladder { border-top: 1px solid var(--border); }
.oi-wall .ladder-row {
  display: grid; grid-template-columns: 64px 1fr; align-items: center;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border); font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 11px;
  height: 24px;
}
.oi-wall .ladder-row.hot { background: rgba(232,184,74,0.08); }
.oi-wall .strike-label { text-align: right; padding-right: 8px; color: var(--ink); font-weight: 500; font-size: 10.5px; }
.oi-wall .bar-cell { position: relative; height: 100%; display: flex; align-items: center; }
.oi-wall .bar { height: 14px; border-radius: 2px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: flex-start; padding-left: 5px; font-size: 9.5px; color: #fff; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; }
.oi-wall .bar.call { background: var(--call-l); color: var(--call-top); }
.oi-wall .bar.put { background: var(--put-l); color: var(--put-top); }
.oi-wall .bar.call.top { background: var(--call); color: #fff; border: 2px solid #E8B84A; height: 16px; font-weight: 700; }
.oi-wall .bar.put.top { background: var(--put); color: #fff; border: 2px solid #E8B84A; height: 16px; font-weight: 700; }
.oi-wall .bar-val { font-size: 9px; color: var(--muted); padding: 0 4px; }

.oi-wall .range-note { font-size: 10.5px; color: var(--muted); text-align: center; padding: 10px 0; }
.oi-wall .legend-toggle { color: var(--accent); cursor: pointer; text-decoration: underline; text-underline-offset: 2px; }

@media (max-width: 480px) {
  .oi-wall .hero-title { font-size: 19px; }
  .oi-wall .stat-grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); }
}
</style>

<div class="oi-wall">
<div class="wrap">
  <div class="hero">
    <div class="hero-eyebrow">Open Interest · 周度更新</div>
    <h1 class="hero-title">科技股周期权OI墙分布图</h1>

  <div class="picker-section">
    <input type="text" class="search-box" id="searchBox" placeholder="搜索标的代码，比如 NVDA / SMCI...">
    <div class="chip-row" id="chipRow"></div>
  </div>

  <div class="panel" id="panel">
    <div class="panel-empty">👆 点一个标的代码，看它的期权OI墙</div>
  </div>
</div>
</div>

false, "ph": false}, {"s": 890.0, "c": 168, "p": 0, "ch": false, "ph": false}, {"s": 900.0, "c": 81, "p": 0, "ch": false, "ph": false}, {"s": 910.0, "c": 530, "p": 0, "ch": false, "ph": false}]}}};
const SNAPSHOT_DATE = "2026-08-13";

let currentSymbol = null;
let currentExpiry = null;
let showFullLadder = false;
let currentOiType = 'call';  // 'call' 或 'put'

function init() {
  document.getElementById('metaDate').textContent = `数据日期 ${SNAPSHOT_DATE}`;
  document.getElementById('metaCount').textContent = `${Object.keys(OI_DATA).length} 只标的`;
  renderChips();
  document.getElementById('searchBox').addEventListener('input', onSearch);
}

function renderChips() {
  const row = document.getElementById('chipRow');
  row.innerHTML = '';
  Object.keys(OI_DATA).sort().forEach(sym => {
    const chip = document.createElement('div');
    chip.className = 'chip';
    chip.textContent = sym;
    chip.dataset.symbol = sym;
    chip.onclick = () => selectSymbol(sym);
    row.appendChild(chip);
  });
}

function onSearch(e) {
  const q = e.target.value.trim().toUpperCase();
  document.querySelectorAll('.chip').forEach(chip => {
    const match = !q || chip.dataset.symbol.includes(q);
    chip.classList.toggle('hidden', !match);
  });
}

function selectSymbol(sym) {
  currentSymbol = sym;
  showFullLadder = false;
  currentOiType = 'call';
  document.querySelectorAll('.chip').forEach(c => {
    c.classList.toggle('active', c.dataset.symbol === sym);
  });
  const expiries = Object.keys(OI_DATA[sym]).sort();
  currentExpiry = expiries[0];
  renderPanel();
}

function selectExpiry(exp) {
  currentExpiry = exp;
  showFullLadder = false;
  renderPanel();
}

function selectOiType(type) {
  currentOiType = type;
  renderPanel();
}

function toggleFullLadder() {
  showFullLadder = !showFullLadder;
  renderPanel();
}

function fmt(n) {
  return n.toLocaleString('en-US');
}

function renderPanel() {
  const panel = document.getElementById('panel');
  if (!currentSymbol) { return; }

  const expiries = Object.keys(OI_DATA[currentSymbol]).sort();
  const d = OI_DATA[currentSymbol][currentExpiry];

  let expiryTabsHtml = '';
  if (expiries.length > 1) {
    expiryTabsHtml = '<div class="expiry-tabs">' + expiries.map(exp => {
      const label = exp.slice(4,6) + '/' + exp.slice(6,8);
      return `<div class="expiry-tab ${exp===currentExpiry?'active':''}" onclick="selectExpiry('${exp}')">${label}到期</div>`;
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }

  const price = d.price;
  const RANGE = 0.35;

  // 按当前Tab（call/put）只保留该方向有持仓的行权价，且按该方向OI值排序展示
  const valKey = currentOiType === 'call' ? 'c' : 'p';
  const hotKey = currentOiType === 'call' ? 'ch' : 'ph';
  let strikes = d.strikes.filter(row => row[valKey] > 0);

  let hiddenCount = 0;
  if (!showFullLadder) {
    const filtered = strikes.filter(row => row.s >= price*(1-RANGE) && row.s <= price*(1+RANGE));
    hiddenCount = strikes.length - filtered.length;
    strikes = filtered;
  }

  const maxOi = Math.max(1, ...strikes.map(row => row[valKey]));
  const barClass = currentOiType; // 'call' 或 'put'

  const ladderRows = strikes.map(row => {
    const val = row[valKey];
    const width = (val / maxOi * 100).toFixed(1);
    const isTop = row[hotKey];
    const rowClass = isTop ? 'hot' : '';
    const showValInside = val >= maxOi * 0.3;
    return `
      <div class="ladder-row ${rowClass}">
        <div class="strike-label">$${row.s}</div>
        <div class="bar-cell">
          <div class="bar ${barClass} ${isTop?'top':''}" style="width:${width}%">${showValInside?fmt(val):''}</div>
          ${!showValInside ? `<span class="bar-val">${fmt(val)}</span>` : ''}
        </div>
      </div>`;
  }).join('');

  const rangeNote = hiddenCount > 0
    ? `<div class="range-note">已收起 ${hiddenCount} 个远虚值行权价（现价±35%范围外） · <span class="legend-toggle" onclick="toggleFullLadder()">展开全部行权价</span></div>`
    : (showFullLadder ? `<div class="range-note"><span class="legend-toggle" onclick="toggleFullLadder()">收起，只看现价附近</span></div>` : '');

  panel.innerHTML = `
    <div class="symbol-head">
      <h2>$${currentSymbol}</h2>
      <span class="price">股价 ~$${d.price}</span>
    </div>
    ${expiryTabsHtml}
    <div class="stat-grid">
      <div class="stat-box"><div class="stat-label">总CALL OI</div><div class="stat-val">${fmt(d.totalCall)}</div></div>
      <div class="stat-box"><div class="stat-label">总PUT OI</div><div class="stat-val">${fmt(d.totalPut)}</div></div>
      <div class="stat-box ratio"><div class="stat-label">Call/Put比</div><div class="stat-val">${d.ratio ?? '—'}</div></div>
      <div class="stat-box"><div class="stat-label">行权价数</div><div class="stat-val">${d.strikes.length}</div></div>
    </div>
    <div class="oi-tabs">
      <div class="oi-tab call ${currentOiType==='call'?'active':''}" onclick="selectOiType('call')">CALL OI<span class="n">${fmt(d.totalCall)}张</span></div>
      <div class="oi-tab put ${currentOiType==='put'?'active':''}" onclick="selectOiType('put')">PUT OI<span class="n">${fmt(d.totalPut)}张</span></div>
    </div>
    <div class="legend">
      <div class="legend-item"><span class="legend-dot regular" style="background:var(--${currentOiType})"></span>普通持仓</div>
      <div class="legend-item"><span class="legend-dot top" style="background:var(--${currentOiType})"></span>TOP3墙（深色）</div>
    </div>
    <div class="ladder">${ladderRows}</div>
    ${rangeNote}
  `;
}

init();

</script>
